Each member signs its approval with an ordinary signature under any EUF-CMA scheme; the quorum jointly produces a threshold seal from Shamir-shared secrets bound to the operation. The seal is the base instance of a programmable authorization computation: simple quorum is the minimal policy, while richer policies can evaluate secret-shared state without making the member-signature scheme part of that computation. The signature scheme is a deployment parameter: migrating from ECDSA to SLH-DSA or ML-DSA is a key rotation, not a protocol redesign, and members holding keys in commodity HSMs participate through the standard sign API. The architecture can be deployed wherever the asset-control path supports programmable verification, such as smart contracts, vault modules, or HSMs guarding a master key, and produces an enforcement-layer authorization rather than a native chain signature. Below-threshold secrecy is information-theoretic; an adversary holding $\geq t$ signing keys but no coefficient shares still cannot produce the seal.

</details>


### [61] [Simulating the Resident: Generating Executable Smart Home Schedules via LLM Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08231)
*Victor Jüttner,Xenia Wagner,Christoph Jahn,Erik Buchmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型生成多样化的居民角色，使其与模拟智能家居交互，生成基于行为规律的可执行设备交互计划，以替代真实家庭数据采集，实现可扩展且保护隐私的智能家居实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能家居的可用安全与隐私研究需要真实环境下的交互数据，但真实数据采集成本高、速度慢且隐私风险大，需要一种不依赖侵入式数据收集的可扩展实验方案。

Method: 提出了一个包含三个组成部分的方法：(1) 一个跨五个社会技术维度配置模拟家庭的设计框架；(2) 一个多阶段LLM流水线，生成结构化的可执行设备交互计划；(3) 一个概念验证以证明可行性。

Result: 完成了概念验证，证明所提方法在技术上是可行的，能够生成基于行为规律的设备交互计划。

Conclusion: 该方法有望在不依赖侵入式真实数据收集的前提下，支持可扩展、注重隐私的智能家居实验研究。

Abstract: Smart homes have emerged as an important domain for HCI research, including work on usable security and privacy. Ideally, studies in these areas draw on datasets collected in real homes with real residents, capturing authentic device interactions, network traffic, and daily routines. However, creating such datasets is slow, expensive, and raises significant privacy concerns, as it requires long-term observation of people in their most private spaces. We propose using LLMs to generate diverse resident personas that interact with a simulated smart home, producing behaviorally grounded interaction schedules that can be executed on physical testbeds. We present (1) a design framework configuring simulated households across five socio-technical dimensions, (2) a multi-stage LLM pipeline that produces structured, executable device interaction schedules, and (3) a proof of concept demonstrating feasibility. As a work in progress, we aim to support scalable, privacy-conscious smart-home experimentation without relying on intrusive real-world data collection.

</details>


### [62] [Mini-Programs, Mega-Problems: Unveiling OAuth-based Authentication Misuses in Mini-Programs via Dynamic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08232)
*Zidong Zhang,Zhentao Xie,Lingyun Ying,Qinsheng Hou,Yacong Gu,Wenrui Diao,Jianliang Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现小程序中OAuth认证流程存在三类运行时误用漏洞（不同于以往静态代码分析），提出了首个动态分析框架MINIAUTH，对44,273个微信小程序和2,721个百度小程序进行分析，发现1,834个误用案例，包括客户端身份伪造、认证绕过等严重安全缺陷，并发现百度OBA API的密码学设计缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅关注静态代码层面的OAuth集成缺陷，但小程序运行时动态行为（如代码混淆、动态加载）使得静态方法无法检测实际执行中产生的认证误用，亟需一种能动态分析小程序OBA流程的自动化检测方法。

Method: 设计并实现MINIAUTH分析框架：自动定位小程序OBA登录页面，动态执行登录工作流，分析运行时行为，从而处理混淆代码并发现动态漏洞。

Result: 对44,273个微信小程序和2,721个百度小程序进行分析，发现1,834个误用案例；包括客户端身份伪造（泄露凭证）、认证绕过（使用静态/明文标识符）等关键逻辑缺陷；并发现百度OBA API中可暴力破解会话密钥的密码学设计缺陷。

Conclusion: OBA误用问题在小程序生态中普遍存在且跨平台一致，需要更完善的开发者指导与平台级安全防护措施。已向开发者和平台负责任披露，获得致谢及CNVD/CNNVD编号。

Abstract: Mini-programs have become a dominant paradigm for lightweight application deployment within super apps such as WeChat. To support seamless integration, super apps provide OAuth mechanisms for user login. However, improper integration of OAuth-based Authentication (OBA) flows by third-party developers can lead to critical security flaws. In this paper, we discover three new types of runtime OBA misuses that differ from prior static-code-based studies, enabling attackers to impersonate victims. To assess their real-world impact, we design and implement MINIAUTH, the first analysis framework for systematically analyzing OBA misuse at scale. MINIAUTH automatically pinpoints the OBA login page of a mini-program, executes the workflow dynamically, and analyzes its runtime behaviors. This enables it to handle obfuscated mini-programs and uncover vulnerabilities that existing approaches cannot detect. Applying MINIAUTH to 44,273 WeChat and 2,721 Baidu mini-programs, we uncover 1,834 misuse cases, including critical logic flaws that enable client-side identity forgery via exposed credentials and authentication bypass through static or plaintext identifiers. Our cross-platform evaluation further shows that such misuses are not confined to a single ecosystem but consistently appear across different mini-program platforms. We also identify a cryptographic design flaw in Baidu's OBA APIs that allows brute-forcing of session keys. We responsibly disclosed our findings to the developers and platforms, receiving acknowledgments and assigned CNVD/CNNVD IDs. These results underscore the need for more robust developer guidance and enhanced platform-level safeguards.

</details>


### [63] [Multi-Agent Firewall Architecture for Privacy Protection of Sensitive Data in Interactions with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08282)
*Hugo García Cuesta,Pablo Mateo Torrejón,Alfonso Sánchez-Macián*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向LLM交互的开源、隐私保护的用户端防火墙，结合浏览器扩展和代理实现全流量拦截，采用多智能体管道进行数据泄露防护，在最优配置下F1分数达94.93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已成为重要生产力工具，但将其集成到工作流中缺乏充分的安全防护措施，存在显著风险，亟需一种面向用户端的防火墙解决方案。

Method: 提出开源、隐私优先的用户端防火墙架构，包含浏览器扩展和代理两个组件，实现对HTTP(S)和WebSocket通信的全流量拦截；核心采用灵活的多智能体管道，通过结合确定性检测器与LLM驱动的语义分析的混合方法实现数据泄露预防，支持专有代码泄露防护和可扩展组件设计。

Result: 在最优配置下，该框架实现了高达94.93%的F1分数。

Conclusion: 该防火墙的分层架构支持异构环境部署，使组织能够在计算成本、检测深度和延迟之间取得平衡，有效保障LLM交互安全。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have become essential productivity tools, their integration into workflows without adequate safeguards creates significant risks. This paper proposes an open-source, privacy-focused, user-facing firewall designed to secure both web-based and programmatic LLM interactions. The architecture combines a browser extension and a proxy for total traffic interception across both HTTP(S) and WebSocket communications. At its core, a flexible multi-agent pipeline delivers data leakage prevention through a hybrid approach combining deterministic detectors with LLM-driven semantic analysis, proprietary code leakage prevention, and extensible components designed for future security enhancements such as prompt injection evasion. The framework's layered architecture enables deployment across heterogeneous environments, allowing organizations to balance computational cost, detection depth and latency. Evaluation results demonstrate it achieves F1 scores of up to 94.93% on optimal configurations.

</details>


### [64] [From Legacy Documentation to OSCAL: An MCP-Based Agent Pipeline for Threat-Informed Continuous Compliance in Critical Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08288)
*Lea Roxanne Muth,Marian Margraf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MCP（Model Context Protocol）的非侵入式多智能体流水线，将自然语言系统描述转换为知识图谱和符合NIST OSCAL标准的合规审计工件，用于关键基础设施的持续自动化合规管理。在水务设施的合成场景中，实现了0.90的CVE召回率和完美的D3FEND召回率，但核心发现是MCP接地并未完全消除幻觉，而是将错误转移到资产提取阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施中的操作技术（OT）环境通常无法被主动扫描，但又需要主动的系统反馈来进行风险评估和合规性检查。现有方法面临可扫描性限制和LLM幻觉风险的双重挑战。

Method: 提出了一种MCP接地（Model Context Protocol grounding）的多智能体流水线架构，将LLM推理与确定性知识检索解耦。流水线首先从自然语言描述中提取资产，然后通过MCP对接权威威胁情报源进行验证，最终生成知识图谱和NIST OSCAL格式的合规工件。

Result: 在供水设施的合成场景中，CVE召回率达到0.90，D3FEND召回率达到100%。生成了符合模式的OSCAL系统安全计划（SSP）和安全评估报告（SAR）。实验表明错误并未被消除，而是转移到资产提取阶段——一个错误的实体提取可能导致后续阶段生成真实但不相关的CVE。

Conclusion: MCP接地不能完全消除流水线中的错误（如幻觉），但能将错误限制在资产提取阶段，使剩余风险变得可见、可验证，便于人工高效审查。由于基础设施信息（如版本号、操作系统等）通常是已知的，这种方法具有实用价值。

Abstract: In critical infrastructure, operational technology environments often cannot be actively scanned, and yet active system feedback is needed for risk assessment and compliance. This paper presents a non-invasive, MCP-grounded multi-agent pipeline that converts natural-language system descriptions into source-verified knowledge graph and audit-ready artifacts in the NIST OSCAL format for continuous automated compliance management. The architecture decouples LLM-based reasoning from deterministic knowledge retrieval against authoritative threat-intelligence sources, reducing the risk of fabricated vulnerabilities and hallucinated attack paths.
  In an evidence-based synthetic scenario of a water utility, the pipeline achieves 0.90 CVE recall and perfect D3FEND recall. It generates a schema-valid OSCAL System Security Plan and an OSCAL Security Assessment Report. Nevertheless, the core insight is not that grounding via MCP eliminates errors (e.g., hallucinations) entirely from the pipeline, but that it shifts errors into the first phase of asset extraction from the natural language description. Here, a single incorrectly extracted entity can lead to genuine but irrelevant CVEs in subsequent stages of the pipeline, which consumes time and resources. However, it makes the remaining risk visible, verifiable, and suitable for a time-efficient manual review, since the infrastructure (e.g., version numbers, OS, etc.) is typically known.

</details>


### [65] [Reverse Engineering Compliance: A Dual-Graph Verification Framework for Auditing Legacy IT Security Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08292)
*Lea Roxanne Muth,Marian Margraf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ASSERT框架，通过本体提取、五类图差异分析和OSCAL导出，解决NIS-2合规要求下遗留IT安全概念文档向机器可读格式迁移时的验证问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIS-2指令要求持续、可审计的合规证据，推动从文档式合规向机器可读合规工件转变。OSCAL是相关标准，但企业仍管理大量遗留IT安全概念文档，直接迁移可能导致过时资产进入新格式。现有研究主要关注新概念的生成，缺乏将遗留文档提取为可审计中间表示、确定性比较图差异并导出合规OSCAL工件的验证框架。

Method: 提出ASSERT框架，包含三个核心步骤：(1) 基于本体的遗留文档提取，构建形式化文档图；(2) 五类图差异分析，与已验证的参考图进行对比；(3) 导出符合schema的OSCAL输出，用于系统描述和评估证据。使用BSI的RecPlast数据集，比较了本地开源模型和商业模型在三种不同参考本体暴露配置下的表现。

Result: ASSERT能够量化文档-基础设施的不一致性，但发现未记录实体与强制执行schema之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: ASSERT框架有效弥合了遗留IT安全概念向OSCAL标准格式迁移过程中的验证缺口，使文档与基础设施间的不一致变得可量化，但实际部署中需平衡发现能力和模式合规性。

Abstract: The NIS-2 Directive increases the need for continuous, auditable compliance evidence and motivates a shift from document-based compliance toward machine-readable compliance artifacts. The Open Security Controls Assessment Language (OSCAL) is a standard for this purpose, which the German Federal Office for Information Security (BSI) is adapting with Grundschutz++. However, companies are still managing extensive legacy IT security concepts (IT-SCs), and migrating them without verification could transfer outdated assets into the new format. While existing research primarily addresses the generation of new concepts, there is a lack of a verification framework that extracts legacy IT-SCs into an auditable intermediate representation, deterministically compares the extracted graph with an independently constructed reference state, and exports schema-valid OSCAL artifacts.
  This paper introduces the Automated Security Concept Structure Extraction and Reverse Topology-checking (ASSERT) Framework, which addresses this gap by using ontology-based extraction of legacy documents into formal document graphs, a five-class graph difference against a verified reference graph, and the export into schema-valid OSCAL outputs for system description and assessment evidence. Using the BSI's RecPlast dataset, we compare a local open-weight model and a commercial model across three configurations with different levels of reference-ontology exposure. The evaluation shows that ASSERT makes document-infrastructure inconsistencies measurable, but reveals a trade-off between discovering undocumented entities and enforcing a schema.

</details>


### [66] [Secure QR Codes: Authenticity Verification via EdDSA Signatures and CBOR Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08383)
*Wojciech Jonderko,Wojciech Wodo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种安全的QR码架构：基于EdDSA签名的完全离线方案（利用Ed25519曲线、CBOR证书和ZLIB压缩实现加密完整性）和面向大规模IoT部署的混合Web PKI架构（通过JWKS端点、中央信任注册表和URL片段实现动态实时验证与向后兼容），以消除QR码欺骗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QR码缺乏固有安全机制，在公共基础设施（如自助停车缴费机）上易受欺骗攻击（quishing），亟需可部署的加密安全方案。

Method: 先提出完全离线方案：使用EdDSA签名（Ed25519曲线）、CBOR编码证书和ZLIB压缩在QR码静态容量内实现加密完整性；再提出混合Web PKI架构：利用JWKS端点、中央信任注册表和URL片段，兼顾标准相机兼容性与合规应用的动态实时验证。

Result: 离线方案证明可在QR码严格容量限制内实现加密完整性；混合架构解决了离线模型在智能城市IoT大规模部署中无法即时密钥撤销的可扩展性限制。

Conclusion: 双模式方法为消除QR码欺骗提供了实际可部署的路径，兼顾安全性与向后兼容性。

Abstract: QR codes are a ubiquitous part of daily life, widely trusted by millions. However, their lack of inherent security features has given rise to critical attack vectors, such as spoofing (quishing) on public infrastructure like self-service parking machines. To address this, we present a comprehensive evolution of secure QR code architectures. First, we evaluate a fully offline proof-of-concept leveraging EdDSA signatures (instantiated on the Ed25519 curve), CBOR-encoded certificates, and ZLIB compression, demonstrating that robust cryptographic integrity can be achieved within the QR code's strict static capacity. However, recognizing the scalability limitations of fully offline models-specifically the inability to perform immediate key revocation in massive smart-city IoT deployments-we subsequently propose a scalable Hybrid Web PKI architecture. This forward-looking model utilizes standardized JWKS endpoints, a Central Trust Registry, and URL fragments to ensure seamless backward compatibility with standard native cameras while providing dynamic, real-time validation for compliant applications. This dual-mode approach offers a practical, deployable path toward eliminating QR spoofing.

</details>


### [67] [Token-Flow Firewall: Semantic Runtime Auditing for Persistent AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08395)
*Puji Wang,Yingchen Zhang,Ruqing Zhang,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TokenWall是一种针对持久化AI智能体的运行时防御框架，通过语义防火墙对智能体的自然语言token流进行边界感知审计，在CIK-Bench上实现了12.5%的攻击成功率与97.4%的良性通过率，仅引入0.69秒额外延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化AI智能体相较于传统聊天助手具有更大的语义攻击面，不安全内容可通过持久状态、可复用技能和工具交互传播，而现有方法依赖稀疏审计或远程大模型监督，缺乏高效的运行时安全防护。

Method: 提出TokenWall框架，作为语义防火墙对智能体的token流（包括记忆更新、工具参数、检索文件和组件间通信）进行边界感知审计，构建结构化源-汇审计记录，执行前进行轻量级局部检查，对模糊高危险情升级至更强仲裁模块。

Result: 在CIK-Bench上，攻击成功率降至12.5%，良性可执行通过率保持97.4%，无需人工确认；良性用例仅引入0.69秒额外延迟。

Conclusion: 语义运行时拦截能够实现持久化AI智能体的实用性安全-效用权衡，全覆盖执行前中介方案在效率和安全性上优于稀疏审计或远程大模型监督。

Abstract: Persistent AI agents extend large language models (LLMs) beyond single-turn interaction into long-lived software systems. Unlike traditional chat assistants, unsafe content in these agents can propagate through persistent state, reusable skills, and tool-mediated interactions, creating a substantially larger semantic attack surface. We observe that most security-critical interactions in such agents are transmitted through natural-language token flows, including memory updates, tool arguments, retrieved files, and inter-component communications. This observation enables a new security formulation: unsafe behavior can be intercepted as risky semantic flows before reaching privileged runtime sinks. Based on this insight, we propose TokenWall, a runtime defense framework that acts as a semantic firewall over agent token flows. TokenWall performs boundary-aware semantic auditing over these flows, constructing structured source-sink audit records, applying lightweight local inspection before execution, and selectively escalating ambiguous high-risk cases to stronger arbitration modules. Unlike prior approaches that rely on sparse auditing or remote large-model oversight, TokenWall enables full-coverage pre-execution mediation while reducing remote arbitration and latency. Experiments on CIK-Bench show that TokenWall reduces attack success rate to 12.5% while maintaining a 97.4% benign executable pass rate without human confirmation. TokenWall further introduces only 0.69 seconds of additional latency on benign cases, demonstrating that semantic runtime containment can achieve a practical security-utility trade-off for persistent AI agents.

</details>


### [68] [TRACE: A Two-Channel Robust Attribution Watermark via Complementary Embeddings for LLM-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08400)
*Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Xiaoyan Feng,Jiaojiao Jiang,Yang Song,Yulei Sui,Zhenchang Xing,Liming Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TRACE提出了一种针对LLM代理的轨迹水印方法，通过叠加选择通道（基于内容键控、无失真采样）和计数通道（基于位置键控）实现对抗删除和重写的无失真、自同步且不变的水印，实验在ToolBench和ALFWorld上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理水印无法抵御具有完整读写轨迹日志权限的中间商攻击，因为水印直接存储在日志中可被篡改；需要一种能对抗删除和重写攻击的水印方案。

Method: TRACE叠加两个水印通道：选择通道基于局部内容键控，使用无失真采样器决定动作选择，在删除后能重新同步；计数通道基于日志骨架（位置）键控，决定每个决策组包含的记录数，重写无法改变。

Result: 在ToolBench和ALFWorld上，TRACE保持与未水印代理相同的成功率；选择通道在长轨迹上检测分数接近z=100，在70%步骤删除下仍可检测；计数通道在任意强度的LLM重写下保持不变。

Conclusion: TRACE是首个满足无失真、自同步抗删除、无条件下抗重写的代理水印，证明了行为水印的信号以决策熵为代价，每次决策至少付出半数熵，确定性决策无代价，擦除两个通道会迫使中间商破坏其转售的轨迹。

Abstract: LLM agents reach users through resellers, who may rebrand a developer's agent or substitute a cheaper model. When provenance is disputed, attribution rests on the trajectory log (the record of tool calls, observations, and executed actions, not the model's reasoning), which the reseller stores and processes to meter usage. A watermark must therefore survive an adversary with full read/write access to the very evidence it is detected from; existing agent watermarks do not, as their attribution is read straight off that log. We present TRACE, to our knowledge the first agent watermark that is distortion-free in its action choices, self-synchronizing under deletion, and unconditionally invariant under rewriting. Deletion desynchronizes a position-derived key and rewriting alters content, so a deletion-robust key must come from content and a rewrite-robust key from position, and no single key serves both. A trajectory, however, has room for two watermarks. TRACE superposes a selection channel that sets which action is chosen, keyed on local content with a distortion-free sampler, so the agent's distribution is provably unchanged and detection resynchronizes after deletions, and a tally channel that sets how many records each decision group holds, keyed on the log's skeleton alone, which no rewriting can touch. We prove this behavioral watermark's signal is bought with decision entropy, each decision paying at least half its entropy and deterministic decisions nothing, and that erasing both channels forces the reseller to corrupt the trajectories it resells. On ToolBench and ALFWorld, TRACE matches the unwatermarked agent's success rate while its selection channel reaches detection scores near z = 100 on long-horizon trajectories, stays detectable under 70% step deletion, and keeps a tally channel exactly unchanged under LLM rewriting of any strength.

</details>


### [69] [TRM-Raft: A Byzantine-Resistant Raft Consensus via Integrated Trust and Reputation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08666)
*Jie Zhang,Xubo Fan,Xiaohong Li,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TRM-Raft 是一种基于区块链信任与声誉模型（B-TRM）的非侵入式拜占庭容错增强方案，通过在 Raft 共识核心中嵌入多维行为量化、自适应惩罚和声誉感知的领导者选举与日志复制机制，在保持低开销的同时有效抵御选举伪造和日志篡改等拜占庭行为。在 Hyperledger Fabric 上的实验表明，即使存在 40% 的拜占庭节点，恶意领导者比例仍低于 5%，吞吐量损失小于 10%，延迟增加小于 5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Raft 共识协议仅假设崩溃故障，易受选举伪造、日志篡改等拜占庭行为攻击；现有 BFT 协议开销过高，而临时性加固缺乏统一防御，亟需一种轻量级、实用的拜占庭容错增强方案。

Method: 将区块链信任与声誉模型（B-TRM）非侵入式地集成到 Raft 共识核心，包括：多维节点行为量化、区分意外故障与恶意攻击的自适应惩罚机制、声誉感知的领导者选举（惩罚任期/索引伪造，排除低声誉节点）以及基于 Schnorr 签名的日志完整性验证（篡改触发声誉衰减和领导者替换）。

Result: 在模拟真实网构软件环境的 Hyperledger Fabric 上评估，TRM-Raft 在 40% 拜占庭节点比例下仍将恶意领导者比例控制在 5% 以下，相比原生 Raft 吞吐量损失小于 10%，延迟增加小于 5%。

Conclusion: TRM-Raft 为依赖 Raft 的网构软件系统提供了一条轻量级、实用的可信增强路径，在有效抵御拜占庭行为的同时保持低性能开销。

Abstract: Internetware envisions autonomous software entities collaborating over the open Internet. Raft consensus is widely adopted for its simplicity and performance in distributed coordination, e.g., service registries and blockchains. However, Raft assumes crash faults only, making it vulnerable to Byzantine behaviors like election forgery and log tampering. Existing BFT protocols incur high overhead, while ad-hoc hardening lacks unified defense.
  We propose \textbf{TRM-Raft}, a Byzantine-resistant enhancement that non-intrusively integrates a Blockchain-based Trust and Reputation Model (B-TRM) into the consensus core. It quantifies multi-dimensional node behaviors, applies adaptive penalties distinguishing accidental faults from malice, and embeds reputation into leader election and log replication. A reputation-aware election penalizes term/index forgery, excluding low-reputation nodes from leadership. A Schnorr-signature-based mechanism lets followers verify log integrity; tampering triggers reputation decay and leader replacement. Evaluated on Hyperledger Fabric in a realistic Internetware setting, TRM-Raft keeps malicious leader ratio below 5\% even with 40\% Byzantine nodes, with <10\% throughput loss and <5\% latency increase over vanilla Raft. TRM-Raft offers a lightweight, practical trustworthiness path for Internetware systems relying on Raft.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [70] [Context Graphs for Proactive Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07721)
*Avinash Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Context Graph的主动式AI代理系统，通过持续监控企业实体状态变化、计算主动性分数并利用LLM生成可操作通知，在合同管理、工程事件响应和销售管线场景中实现了0.83的Precision@5和低于30秒的预警时间，优于传统被动式RAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG和代理框架本质上是反应式的——必须等待用户查询才能行动，无法实现真正的企业生产力提升。本文论证了企业需要能够在工人提问之前主动推送相关可操作信息的主动式代理系统。

Method: 提出Context Graph（实时关系数据结构建模企业实体及其关系和状态变化）、Delta Detection Engine（持续监控状态变化）、Proactivity Scorer（根据紧迫性、相关性和角色匹配度对候选洞察排序）以及LLM驱动的Surfacing Layer（生成带依据说明的排名通知），并给出统一的Proactivity Score函数和端到端Python实现。

Result: 在合同生命周期管理、工程事件响应和销售管线卫生三个通用企业案例中，Precision@5达0.83，假阳性率0.11，平均预警时间从反应式基线的47分钟降至30秒以下。

Conclusion: 基于Context Graph的主动式代理框架能显著提升企业AI系统的生产力和响应速度，从被动等待查询转变为主动推送洞察。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic frameworks have advanced enterprise AI considerably, yet agents remain fundamentally reactive: they wait for a human query before acting. This paper argues that genuine enterprise productivity gains require proactive agents: systems that surface relevant, actionable information to workers before they ask. We propose the Context Graph, a live relational data structure that models enterprise entities, their relationships, and state transitions over time. Built on this graph, we define a Delta Detection Engine that continuously monitors state changes, a Proactivity Scorer that ranks candidate insights by urgency, relevance, and persona-fit, and a Surfacing Layer powered by an LLM that delivers ranked notifications with grounded explanations. We formalize each component, derive a unified Proactivity Score function, and provide a complete end-to-end Python implementation using NetworkX and the Anthropic Claude API. Evaluation across three generic enterprise case studies (contract lifecycle management, engineering incident response, and sales pipeline hygiene) demonstrates that context-graph-driven proactivity achieves Precision@5 of 0.83, a false positive rate of 0.11, and reduces mean time to surface from 47 minutes (reactive baseline) to under 30 second.

</details>


### [71] [AI-integrated models for assessing agricultural resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07759)
*Joshua R. Waite,Dana Golden,Brett Indelicato,Kevin Camp,Mojdeh Saadati,Shannon Regan,Patrick Schnable,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian,Carlos Messina,Suzanne Thornsbury,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个AI驱动的工具，将经济模型(GTAP)与生物物理模型(APSIM)集成，通过自然语言问答方式帮助政策制定者和市场参与者评估农业供应链冲击的跨学科影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业供应链容易受到生物物理和经济系统耦合中断的影响，现有方法难以跨学科评估供应链冲击的连锁反应，需要一种能够整合多领域模型并以自然语言交互的工具。

Method: 开发AI驱动工具，集成GTAP（全球经济贸易分析模型）和APSIM（农业生产力模拟器）两种模型，支持用户通过自然语言查询和回答来评估供应链冲击的跨学科影响。

Result: 实现了一个能够整合经济与生物物理模型的AI工具，使政策制定者和市场参与者能够以自然语言方式获取跨学科供应链冲击分析结果。

Conclusion: AI驱动的模型集成方法可有效弥合经济与生物物理系统之间的分析鸿沟，为农业供应链韧性管理提供跨学科决策支持。

Abstract: Agricultural supply chains are vulnerable to disruptions through linked biophysical and economic systems. We develop an AI-powered tool that integrates economic models (GTAP) with biophysical models (APSIM) to analyze supply chain shocks, enabling policymakers and market participants to assess cross-disciplinary impacts through queries and responses written in natural language.

</details>


### [72] [Adversarial Social Epistemology for Assemblies of Humans and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07760)
*Mihnea C. Moldoveanu,Joel A. C. Baum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对抗性社会认识论（ASE），用于分析密集交互的传播环境中，行为者如何利用正常使断言可信的承诺与权利，通过扭曲、省略、伪造信息等手段破坏基于证词链、推理、制度认证和隐性信任构建的公共传播系统的可信性，并提供了审计和补救信任破裂的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念（如信息茧房、回音室、 misinformation 传播）不足以解释在密集交互的传播环境中，行为者如何系统性地利用使断言可信的承诺与权利来破坏信任。

Method: 基于推理主义语义学，结合认识论网络，提出对抗性社会认识论（ASE），分析行为者如何通过破坏推断链的可审计性来颠覆信任，并设计审计与修复机制。

Result: 提供了能够精确分析上述现象的语言框架，描述了颠覆公开传播信任的具体机制，并建立了用于审计和补救因破坏推断链可审计性而导致的信任破裂的工具。

Conclusion: 对抗性社会认识论（ASE）为理解现代高度互动的传播环境中系统性的信任破坏行为提供了新的分析框架和应对方案。

Abstract: We outline an adversarial social epistemology (ASE) for densely interactive communicative landscapes in which public assertions are scaffolded by chains of testimony, inference, institutional certification, and tacit trust. In such landscapes, agents have incentives and affordances to distort, color, omit, fabricate, or strategically under-specify information for private, reputational, rhetorical, or material gains. We argue that these phenomena are not adequately captured by familiar descriptions of epistemic bubbles, echo chambers, or misinformation diffusion. What requires explanation is how communicative agents exploit the commitments and entitlements that normally make scaffolded assertions trustworthy. We provide language that delivers the requisite analysis, outline mechanisms that subvert trust in scaffolded public communications, and outline machinery for auditing and redressing trust breaches arising from subverting the auditability of inferential chains, drawing on epistemic networks, enriched with an inferentialist semantics for interpreting assertions.

</details>


### [73] [Aligning Clinical Needs and AI Capabilities: A Survey on LLMs for Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07761)
*Qi Peng,Jiatong Li,Sirui Huang,Yiyang Jiang,Kaisong Gong,Ronger Ding,Shijie Ye,Changmeng Zheng,Yi Cai,Xiaobo Yang,Jin Huang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了医学大语言模型的最新进展，提出了一种连接临床实践与计算方法的双视角框架，建立了基于Miller金字塔的五级能力体系，并在18个先进模型上进行了基准测试，揭示了不同模型在诊断、决策支持等任务中的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs在医疗领域的广泛应用，需要系统性地梳理和评估其在临床推理方面的能力，建立连接临床实践与计算方法的理论框架，以指导更安全、可靠的医学AI系统开发。

Method: 采用双视角方法：临床侧基于Miller金字塔建立五级能力体系（从知识回忆到动态病例管理）；计算侧将演绎、归纳、溯因推理模式与常见医疗目标和任务关联；构建跨越五级推理能力的基准数据集，在18个SOTA模型上进行评估。

Result: 医学专科模型在诊断类任务中表现优异，而通用模型在决策支持和对话任务中领先。揭示了数据局限性、幻觉问题和接地问题等当前主要挑战。

Conclusion: 医疗LLMs取得显著进展但面临数据限制、幻觉和接地等挑战，未来需朝着更安全、更可靠、更适配工作流的系统方向努力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as important tools in healthcare, showing growing potential for clinical reasoning and patient care. This survey examines recent progress in medical LLMs, focusing on reasoning applications and requirements. We present a dual-view approach that connects clinical practice with computational methods. On the clinical side, we establish a five-level competency scheme following Miller's Pyramid, progressing from knowledge recall to dynamic case management. On the computational side, we link deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning patterns to common medical goals and tasks. We also introduce a benchmark dataset spanning five levels of medical reasoning capability and report results on 18 state-of-the-art models, revealing that medical specialist models excel in diagnosis-centric tasks while general models lead in decision support and dialogue. We conclude by discussing current progress and open challenges, including data limitations, hallucination, and grounding issues, and outline directions toward safer, more reliable, and workflow-ready systems.

</details>


### [74] [Alignment Plausibility: A New Standard for Assuring AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07766)
*Gwydion Williams,Sara Zannone,Bilal A Mateen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将大型语言模型在心理健康支持中的安全性问题，通过类比临床实践的三大保障层次（价值规范、训练嵌入、部署监督），构建"对齐可信度"（alignment plausibility）这一监管概念，用以系统论证AI系统是否真正对齐积极健康结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在心理健康支持中应用广泛，但其注意力经济模式优先追求持续参与而非有效心理干预所需的"摩擦"；现有安全响应多是被动应对显性、急性伤害，而对依赖、边界侵蚀、扭曲信念放大等隐匿长期风险关注不足。

Method: 提出对齐的三层次框架（价值规范→训练嵌入→部署监督），并类比临床实践中的安全保障机制；引入"对齐可信度"概念，类比生物学中的"生物学可信度"（biological plausibility），作为监管性建构。

Result: 构建了"对齐可信度"这一概念，用于结构化展示系统在价值观、训练机制和监督机制上的一致性与安全性，可作为论证AI系统是否值得信任、是否无害、是否促进患者福祉的规范性工具。

Conclusion: 需要通过多层次对齐框架来实现LLM的结构化安全，对齐可信度可作为AI健康领域的监管概念，为信任和安全评估提供原则性路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become significant providers of mental health support, yet they remain products of an attention economy whose operational and commercial targets favour sustained engagement over the friction that effective psychological support often requires. Developers' safety responses have been largely reactive, addressing the most visible and acute harms while subtler, longer-term patterns of risk (e.g., dependency, boundary erosion, the amplification of distorted beliefs) receive less attention. We contend that making LLMs structurally safe requires alignment organised at three levels that mirror how society assures the safety of human clinical practice: 1) explicit value specification grounded in the codified normative commitments of clinical practice; 2) training that embeds those values in the model; and 3) oversight that detects drift and longer-term harm during deployment, much as clinical supervision does for human practice. Organising alignment in this way yields a construct we call alignment plausibility - a structured demonstration that a system's values, training regime, and oversight mechanisms are together consistent with safe and positive outcomes. We propose alignment plausibility as a regulatory construct (by drawing analogy to the established construct of biological plausibility) for AI in health: a principled way to argue for, or against, trust that systems are aligned to positive health outcomes, will cause no harm even where capable of doing so, and will ultimately lead to patient benefit.

</details>


### [75] [Idiobionics: The Unification of Privacy and Intelligent Robotic Prostheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07775)
*Kwesi Afari Darfoor,Patrick M. Pilarski,Bailey Kacsmar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"idiobionics"这一新研究方向，系统研究智能仿生肢体的隐私风险，通过定义该领域、梳理相关文献、展示潜在对抗性攻击并列出开放研究问题，旨在解决隐私问题以充分释放仿生肢体的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能仿生肢体（机器人假肢）在感知和控制方面的进步提升了其能力，但同时也引入了可能被恶意实体利用来侵犯用户隐私的攻击向量。为了充分实现下一代仿生肢体的益处，需要直接理解和解决这些隐私风险及其对用户采用的障碍。

Method: 定义了idiobionics这一新研究领域，将其扎根于相关文献中，通过初步证据展示和讨论了可能利用智能仿生肢体设计的潜在对抗性攻击，并梳理了面向可穿戴机器人和其他人机交互自主系统研究者的开放研究问题清单。

Result: 提出了idiobionics的定义，展示了智能仿生肢体可能面临的对抗性攻击初步证据，并整理了一系列相关的开放研究问题。

Conclusion: Idiobionics研究将有助于解锁机器人假肢及相关仿生设备的全部潜力。

Abstract: The human body is at the center of a growing family of technologies designed to tightly and persistently couple biological and digital systems. Robotic prostheses are a representative example of this tight coupling. Also referred to as bionic limbs, robotic prostheses are devices that support people who have lost limbs in pursuing daily life activities such as walking and grasping objects. Bionic limbs are now perceptive and responsive owing to their integration with advanced sensors and artificial intelligence-based control approaches. Consequently, such robotic prostheses can now be viewed as semiautonomous wearable robotic systems that can co-adapt with their users. However, the same sensing and control advancements that increase the capability of robotic prostheses also introduce threat vectors that could be exploited by malicious entities to violate the privacy of users. To fully realize the benefits of next-generation bionic limbs, we maintain it is important to directly understand and address these privacy risks and the barriers they might present to user adoption. This paper therefore introduces a new line of inquiry we term idiobionics to holistically investigate issues at the intersection of privacy and intelligent bionic limbs. As the main contribution of this paper, we define idiobionics, ground it in related literature, and provide preliminary evidence showing and discussing potential adversarial attacks that could exploit intelligent bionic limb designs. We then contribute a curated list of open research questions within idiobionics that are relevant to researchers in wearable robotics and other human-facing autonomous systems. We expect that idiobionics research will help unlock the full potential of robotic prostheses and related bionic devices.

</details>


### [76] [Infinity-Parser2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07836)
*Zuming Huang,Jun Huang,Kexuan Ren,Baode Wang,Weizhen Li,Jianming Feng,Yu Wang,Yichen Yao,Shijun Lin,Yige Tang,Cheng Peng,Weidi Xu,Wei Chu,Yinghui Xu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Infinity-Parser2 是一个结合可控数据合成流水线与多任务强化学习的大型多模态文档解析模型，通过开源5百万双语语料库和八目标联合强化学习，在多个文档解析基准上达到SOTA，并提供了高速版（Flash）和精确版（Pro）两种变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决文档解析领域长期存在的忠实标注语料库稀缺问题，现有数据难以覆盖多样的文档类型和格式，且缺乏大规模高质量的多任务标注数据。

Method: 1) 构建可控渲染框架与迭代精炼循环相结合的可扩展合成引擎，生成5百万样本的双语语料库Infinity-Doc2-5M，包含元素边界框、规范内容形式及全页阅读顺序标注；2) 引入可验证的多任务奖励系统，在八个协同训练目标上实现联合强化学习，统一感知、结构与推理；3) 发布两种变体：Flash版优化低延迟推理（吞吐量提升3.68倍），Pro版面向高精度场景。

Result: Infinity-Parser2-Pro在olmOCR-Bench上达到87.6%，在ParseBench上达到74.3%，超越了DeepSeek-OCR-2、PaddleOCR-VL-1.5和MinerU2.5，并在图表、化学式和文档VQA上表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过可控数据合成与多任务强化学习的结合，Infinity-Parser2有效解决了文档解析中的标注稀缺问题，证明了合成数据+联合RL范式在端到端文档解析中的有效性。

Abstract: We present Infinity-Parser2, a large multimodal model that couples a controllable data-synthesis pipeline with multi-task reinforcement learning for end-to-end document parsing, addressing the persistent scarcity of faithfully annotated parsing corpora. Our contributions are threefold. First, we build a scalable synthesis engine, pairing a controllable rendering framework with an iterative refinement loop, and use it to construct and open-source Infinity-Doc2-5M: a 5-million-sample bilingual (Chinese/English) corpus spanning diverse document types, annotated with element bounding boxes, canonical content forms (Markdown, HTML, LaTeX, SMILES, structured charts), and full-page reading order. Second, we introduce a verifiable, multi-task reward system that enables Joint Reinforcement Learning across eight co-trained objectives (document parsing, layout analysis, table parsing, math formula parsing, chart parsing, chemical formula parsing, document VQA, and general multimodal understanding), unifying perception, structure, and reasoning in a single optimization signal. Third, we release two variants under a shared architecture: Infinity-Parser2-Flash, optimized for low-latency inference with a $3.68\times$ throughput gain over Infinity-Parser-7B, and Infinity-Parser2-Pro, engineered for precision-critical settings. Infinity-Parser2-Pro reaches state-of-the-art 87.6% on olmOCR-Bench and 74.3% on ParseBench, surpassing DeepSeek-OCR-2, PaddleOCR-VL-1.5, and MinerU2.5, with strong generalization to charts, chemical formulas, and document VQA.

</details>


### [77] [VectorizationLLM: Smart Vectorization Based AI Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07846)
*Ryan Duke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VectorizationLLM是一个基于Google开源大模型、专为MATLAB课程（CTEC 247）设计的教学辅助大语言模型，采用RAG知识库和系统提示架构，通过代码、文本和图像示例解释向量化、时频分析、分段函数、傅里叶分析和微分方程概念，但不直接提供答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助学生通过LLM学习智能向量化、时/波向量分析、分段函数、傅里叶分析和微分方程等MATLAB课程内容，同时避免LLM直接给出答案导致学生丧失独立思考能力。

Method: 基于Google开源权重LLM，采用RAG（检索增强生成）知识库结合系统提示架构，在回复中提供代码、文本和图像示例，但不直接给出答案。

Result: 构建了一个专门的教学辅助LLM，能够提供概念讲解和课堂笔记示例，涵盖向量化、时频分析、分段函数、傅里叶分析和微分方程等主题。

Conclusion: VectorizationLLM为NYIT的CTEC 247课程提供了一个智能教学辅助工具，在利用LLM能力帮助学生学习的同时，通过设计约束避免了直接提供答案的问题。

Abstract: VectorizationLLM is a specialized Large Language Model based on Google open-weight LLMs. The model is designed to assist students to learn smart vectorization, time/wave vector analysis, piecewise functions, Fourier analysis, and differential equations in MATLAB. The course application is CTEC 247: Applied Computational Analysis II by the Department of Electrical & Computer Engineering Technology at New York Institute of Technology Old Westbury. The LLM model is designed to be an instructive assistant, providing detailed explanations of concepts with examples from in-class notes without providing direct answers to questions. The model is designed with a RAG (Retrieval Augmented Generation) knowledge base and system prompt architecture. Examples in both code, text, and images are provided in the LLM responses.

</details>


### [78] [A Graph Neural Network Model for Real-Time Gesture Recognition Based on sEMG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07850)
*Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Kamal Premaratne,Manohar Murthi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图网络表示表面肌电信号（sEMG）的新方法，利用图神经网络（GNN）实现实时手部手势识别，在8名健康受试者、8电极Myoband数据上达到99%的平均分类准确率，且图构建与预测平均耗时仅48ms，优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现对先进假手和增强现实的无缝控制，需要准确且实时的的手势识别。现有sEMG手势识别方法在准确率和实时性上仍有提升空间。

Method: 使用图网络对前臂肌肉激活模式进行建模，将sEMG信号编码为图结构，再基于图神经网络（GNN）构建分类算法进行实时手势识别。采用Myoband（8电极）采集8名健康受试者的sEMG信号进行实验评估。

Result: 平均分类准确率达99%，超过现有最先进方法；在M1 Pro CPU上，图构建与预测平均耗时48ms，满足实时应用需求。

Conclusion: 基于图网络的sEMG表示结合GNN的方法在准确率和实时性上均优于现有技术，适用于假手控制和增强现实等实时手势识别场景。

Abstract: For seemless control of advanced hand prostheses and augmented reality, accurate and immediate hand gestures recognition is essential. Surface electromyography (sEMG) signals obtained from the forearm are commonly employed for this purpose. In this paper, we present a novel approach for sEMG representation that utilizes graph networks which contain information about muscle activation patterns in the forearm. Based on these graph networks, we have developed a machine learning algorithm capable of real-time hand gesture recognition using a graph neural network. The algorithm's performance was evaluated using sEMG signals acquired from myoband, which has 8 electrodes placed around the forearm, involving 8 healthy subjects. The proposed method demonstrated an average classification accuracy of 99\%, surpassing the performance of state-of-the-art techniques. The average time for both graph construction and prediction stood at 48ms utilizing a M1 pro CPU, rendering the approach well-suited for real-time applications.

</details>


### [79] [Agentic AI and Retrieval-Augmented Models in Straight-Through Underwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07858)
*Robert Richardson,Josh Meyers,Brian Hartman,David Sandberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了从传统规则到LLM、RAG及多智能体Agentic RAG等架构如何支持精算实务中的透明度、可审计性和人在回路治理，并通过小型商业BOP保单的直通式核保实验证明：在多步骤和缺失信息场景下，Agentic RAG系统表现最佳，能有效避免无依据的直通决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正在重塑精算实务，尤其是在非结构化文档、异构数据源和受监管决策流程领域。精算师面临从传统规则自动化到大语言模型、RAG和多智能体系统的设计空间选择，亟需系统性研究这些新兴架构如何支持透明度、可审计性和人在回路治理等精算核心需求。

Method: 构建合成但真实的实验环境，设计了三种核保流水线：单LLM基线、朴素RAG系统和多智能体Agentic RAG系统（结合定向检索、第三方数据检查和显式多步规则评估），在小型商业BOP保单直通式核保场景下进行对比实验。

Result: Agentic RAG系统整体性能最优，尤其在多步骤和缺失信息场景下提升最大，结构化检索和反思机制有助于模型避免无依据的直通决策。

Conclusion: 多智能体Agentic RAG架构能更好地满足精算领域对透明度、审计性和人在回路治理的要求，为受监管的直通式决策流程提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is beginning to reshape actuarial practice, particularly in domains that require reasoning over unstructured documents, heterogeneous data sources, and regulated decision workflows. Actuaries now face a design space that ranges from traditional rule-based automation to large language models (LLMs), retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and multi-agent ``agentic'' systems that plan, retrieve, call tools, and reflect. This paper examines how these emerging architectures can support actuarial priorities such as transparency, auditability, and human-in-the-loop governance, with a focus on straight-through decision processes. To make these ideas concrete, we develop and analyze an agentic AI framework for straight-through underwriting of small commercial Business Owner Policies (BOPs). We construct a synthetic but realistic experimental environment and compare three underwriting pipelines: (i) a single-LLM baseline, (ii) a naive RAG system, and (iii) a multi-agent ``Agentic RAG'' pipeline that combines targeted retrieval, third-party data checks, and explicit multi-step rule evaluation. The agentic system performs best overall, with the largest gains in multi-step and missing-information scenarios, where structured retrieval and reflection help the model avoid unsupported straight-through decisions.

</details>


### [80] [Feedback Manipulation Regularization: Enabling Offline Agent Alignment for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07859)
*Benjamin Poole,Minwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出反馈操作正则化（FMR）方法，利用评估反馈作为纠错信号改进模仿学习策略的对齐性，在Safety Gymnasium环境中最高减少98%的错位行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法主要在多阶段流水线中结合人类演示和反馈信号，但缺乏在完全序列决策环境中利用这两种互补信号进行单阶段离线训练的研究。

Method: 提出FMR，一种算法无关的正则化方法，将评估反馈作为纠错信号来改善模仿学习策略的对齐性能；并改造Safety Gymnasium环境作为对齐评估的基准测试平台。

Result: 在多种模仿学习算法上展示了对齐能力的提升，最高可减少98%的错位行为；在有限数据场景下（包括稀少对齐样本和无信息噪声演示）仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FMR能有效利用评估反馈信号改善序列决策中模仿学习策略的对齐性，且在不同数据条件下均表现出鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) research has increasingly shifted focus towards alignment, ensuring agents learn behaviors adhering to human values. While human demonstrations and feedback have proven crucial for alignment, existing approaches predominantly combine these signals using multi-stage pipelines designed for the contextual bandit framing of language generation. Yet little work explores how these complementary inputs can serve as a richer, interconnected signal for single-stage offline training in fully sequential decision-making environments. We propose Feedback Manipulation Regularization (FMR), an algorithm-agnostic method that harnesses evaluative feedback as a corrective signal to improve the alignment of imitation learning policies. We adapt Safety Gymnasium environments to be a principled testbed for alignment evaluation, demonstrating improved aptitude and up to a 98\% reduction in misalignment across a range of imitation learning algorithms. FMR remains robust in limited data regimes, even when learning from scarce aligned and uninformative noisy demonstrations.

</details>


### [81] [Nigeria Machinery: A Low-Resource Industrial Dataset with a Domain-Grounded Reasoning Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07883)
*Gospel Bassey,Vincent Fakiyesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作发布了尼日利亚机械使用与故障数据集（89条记录、28个指标，覆盖2006-2025年制造业和油气行业），并开发了一种从稀疏数值构建思维链推理示例的方法，生成了94条提示-完成-推理轨迹数据，解决了语言模型构建数据集时提示与真实领域脱节的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲经济体工业机械的公开、模型可用数据稀缺，导致难以进行定量分析或训练语言模型完成该场景下的数值任务。

Method: 1) 构建尼日利亚机械使用与故障数据集，包含89条机器级记录、28个指标，每条记录标注公开来源并附代码簿；2) 提出从稀疏数值构建思维链推理示例的方法，生成94条提示-完成-推理轨迹，确保提示与真实领域信息绑定。

Result: 领域关联提示占比从早期版本的1/78提升至94/94，检索答案与源值匹配率达84/84；但数据集规模较小（89条记录，其中17个指标仅含单个观测值），多数推理行为为检索而非多步计算。

Conclusion: 该数据集可作为参考和种子数据集使用，但非大规模训练集；同时揭示了语言模型构建数据集时提示与领域脱节的常见问题及解决方案。

Abstract: There is relatively little, public, and model-ready data on industrial machinery for African economies. This makes it hard to do quantitative analysis or to train language models on numeric tasks grounded in that setting. We release two things to help with part of this problem. The first is the Nigeria Machinery Usage and Failures Dataset: 89 machine-level records across 28 indicators, covering Nigeria's manufacturing and oil and gas sectors from 2006 to 2025. Every record names a public source and is decoded by a codebook. The second is a method for building chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning examples from these sparse numeric values. The result is 94 prompt, completion, and reasoning-trace rows. In every row, the prompt names the real indicator, subsector, year, and source of the record it comes from. The data adaptation work was carried out by Adaption Labs. Along the way we describe a problem that is common when language models are used to build datasets. The prompts can match the real numbers while saying nothing about the real domain. We show that fixing this raises the share of domain-grounded prompts from 1 out of 78 in an earlier release to 94 out of 94, and that every retrieval answer now matches its source value (84 out of 84). We release the data, the reasoning layer, and a per-row provenance file under CC-BY-4.0. We are clear about the limits. With 89 records and 17 indicators that have only one observation, this is a reference and seed dataset, not a large training set. Most reasoning rows are retrieval rather than multi-step computation.

</details>


### [82] [Persona Cartography: Charting Language Model Personality Traits in Weight Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07916)
*Luke Baines,Anton Gonzalvez Hawthorne,Mariia Koroliuk,Irakli Shalibashvili,Clément Dumas,Konstantinos Voudouris,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用OCEAN人格框架（开放性、尽责性、外倾性、宜人性、神经质）来分解、测量和控制大语言模型的行为特质，通过训练低秩适配器放大或抑制特定人格特质，发现这些特质适配器具有单调性、可加组合性，并能影响模型的安全相关行为。此外还引入了无监督心理测量管线，从模型生成中恢复出四个可解释的行为因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型表现出重复的行为模式（即人格），这些模式影响泛化能力和安全性，但我们缺乏可靠的工具来分解、测量和控制这些行为模式。

Method: 1) 将模型人格视为行为特质空间中的位置，采用OCEAN框架进行描述；2) 训练低秩适配器（LoRA）来放大或抑制个体特质；3) 使用LLM裁判（经人工验证面板校准）、特质特定的多项选择基准和标准能力评估来评测效果；4) 在6个模型（4B-32B，来自3个模型家族）上进行实验；5) 引入无监督心理测量管线从模型生成中恢复行为因子。

Result: 每个特质适配器在尺度上大致单调地移动目标特质；多个适配器可近似加性组合以构建混合人格；在适度尺度下保持能力基准性能；诱导的特质轴影响下游安全相关行为（如神经质轴影响挫折感，宜人性轴影响谄媚）；无监督心理测量恢复了四个行为因子：语气、主动性、说教性、认知谨慎。

Conclusion: 人格控制可以通过在权重空间中学习、缩放和组合特质来实现，为人格测量、模型编辑和安全性之间搭建了桥梁。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit recurring behavioural patterns -- personas -- that shape generalisation and safety, but we lack reliable tools for decomposing, measuring, and controlling them. Our central insight is to treat personas as positions in a space of behavioural traits, using the OCEAN framework to describe model personas in terms of Openness, Conscientiousness, Extraversion, Agreeableness, and Neuroticism. We train low-rank adapters to amplify or suppress individual traits, and evaluate their effects using an LLM-judge calibrated against a human-validated panel, trait-specific multiple-choice benchmarks, and standard capability evaluations. Across six models from three families (4B-32B), we find that each adapter moves its target trait largely monotonically with scale, combines approximately additively with other adapters to construct mixed personas, and preserves performance on capability benchmarks at moderate scales. We further show that the induced trait axes affect safety-relevant behaviour in downstream evaluations: for example, moving along neuroticism and agreeableness axes affects frustration and sycophancy respectively. We also introduce an unsupervised psychometric pipeline that recovers four interpretable behavioural factors (tone, initiative, didacticism, epistemic caution) from model rollouts. Persona control can then be considered in terms of learning, scaling, and composing traits in weight space, providing a bridge between personality measurement, model editing, and safety.

</details>


### [83] [Evaluating the Effect of Frame Rate in Sequence-Based Classification of Autism-Related Self-Stimulatory Hand Idiosyncrasies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07957)
*Raunak Mondal,Peter Washington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了从视频中自动检测自闭症相关自我刺激行为的两大挑战：最优序列神经网络架构和时间采样率，以及小行为数据集上的数据增强策略。LSTM和GRU模型在SSBD数据集上达到97.5%-98.75%的峰值准确率（每15帧采样），水平翻转是最高独立准确率的数据增强方法（48.78%），而欠采样在增强管道中最关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球超过7500万人受自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）影响，但目前缺乏可扩展的远程行为筛查计算方法，尤其针对自闭症相关的自我刺激行为的自动视频检测仍面临挑战。

Method: 针对两个目标：(1) 在SSBD数据集上，以1、5、15、30、45、90帧为采样间隔，训练LSTM和GRU模型，与CNN基线对比；(2) 在I3D迁移学习管道中应用十种数据增强策略，通过消融实验量化每种技术的边际贡献，并采用个性化机器学习方法（每个受试者独立建模）。

Result: LSTM和GRU峰值准确率分别为97.5%和98.75%（每15帧采样），超过CNN基线（62-76%）。水平翻转独立准确率最高（48.78%），剔除欠采样导致性能下降最大。个性化方法一致性好（平均损失1.84，SD 0.79）。

Conclusion: 为数据稀缺的临床领域中的视频行为分类提供了架构选择、采样率和增强策略的具体实践指导。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) affects over 75 million individuals worldwide, yet scalable computational methods for remote behavioral screening remain limited. This study addresses two complementary challenges in automated detection of autism-related self-stimulatory behaviors from video: (1) identifying the optimal sequence-based neural network architecture and temporal sampling rate, and (2) characterizing data augmentation strategies for training on small behavioral datasets. For the first objective, long short-term memory (LSTM) and gated recurrent unit (GRU) models were trained on pose-derived features from the Self-Stimulatory Behavior Diagnosis (SSBD) dataset at frame sampling intervals of 1, 5, 15, 30, 45, and 90 frames. Both architectures exceeded prior convolutional neural network (CNN) baselines (62-76% accuracy), with peak accuracies of 97.5% (LSTM) and 98.75% (GRU) at a sampling interval of every 15 frames. For the second objective, ten data augmentation strategies were applied to an I3D transfer learning pipeline, with an ablation study quantifying the marginal contribution of each technique. Horizontal flip achieved the highest standalone accuracy (48.78%), while exclusion of upsampling from the augmentation pipeline produced the largest performance degradation, indicating its necessity for complex behavioral video augmentation. A personalized machine learning approach, in which per-subject models were trained and tested on temporally split segments of each video, produced consistent predictions (mean loss 1.84, SD 0.79). These results provide practitioners with concrete guidance on architecture selection, sampling rate, and augmentation strategy for video-based behavioral classification in data-scarce clinical domains.

</details>


### [84] [Agentic Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07984)
*Seokhoon Jeong,Mijung Kim,Taehwan Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentNAS方法，通过LLM生成种子架构并分解为"槽式架构"（带命名、可互换模块槽的框架），自动为传统NAS构建有界且任务特定的搜索空间，无需人工设计。在17个任务上取得11个SOTA，验证了LLM设计+NAS搜索的分工互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS方法受限于需要大量领域知识且必须为每个新任务重新构建的人工搜索空间；而LLM虽能在开放空间中生成架构，但如何最优地分配LLM驱动设计与NAS驱动搜索之间的分工尚未被探索。

Method: 提出AgentNAS，一个模块化三阶段流水线：1）LLM生成高质量种子架构；2）将种子架构分解为"槽式架构"——带命名、可互换模块槽的框架，自动定义有界且任务特定的搜索空间；3）在该搜索空间内执行传统NAS进行组合搜索。

Result: 在NAS-Bench-360和Unseen NAS的17个任务（涵盖分类、密集回归、分割、多标签标注等多种模态）中，AgentNAS在11个任务上达到SOTA，优于包括任务特定专家设计在内的已发表基线。消融实验表明LLM生成种子已超越多数基线，NAS通过槽间组合重组带来额外增益，且该模式独立LLM采样无法复制。

Conclusion: LLM驱动设计与NAS驱动搜索具有广泛互补性，二者分工在不同能力水平的LLM上均表现稳健，为自动化架构设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) methods have grown increasingly efficient, yet they remain bounded by manually engineered search spaces that require substantial domain expertise and must be rebuilt for every new task. Large language models (LLMs) can generate architectures in an open-ended space, but how to optimally divide the labor between LLM-driven design and NAS-driven search remains unexplored. We propose a mechanism that bridges these two paradigms: an LLM produces a high-quality seed architecture, then decomposes it into a "slotted architecture", a scaffold with named, interchangeable module slots that automatically defines a bounded, task-specific search space for conventional NAS to explore, without manual engineering. We instantiate this mechanism in AgentNAS, a modular three-phase pipeline in which each component's contribution can be measured independently. On 17 tasks spanning classification, dense regression, segmentation, and multi-label tagging across diverse modalities (NAS-Bench-360 and Unseen NAS), AgentNAS establishes a new state of the art on 11 tasks, outperforming published baselines including task-specific expert designs. Ablation studies show that the two search mechanisms are broadly complementary: the LLM-generated seed already surpasses published baselines on the majority of tasks, and NAS delivers additional gains in most cases through combinatorial recombination across slots, a mode of search that independent LLM samples cannot replicate. These patterns hold across three LLMs of different capability levels, confirming that the division of labor is robust. Our code is available at https://github.com/alroimfebruary/AgentNAS.

</details>


### [85] [Concretized Proposition Prompting Resolves Composition-Knowledge Dichotomy in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08018)
*Changhun Lee,Minguk Jeon,Jongkyung Shin,Chiehyeon Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出具体化命题提示（CPP）框架，通过显式具体化与问题相关的命题，解决了LLMs在组合性与知识性之间的二元对立问题，显著提升了推理性能，特别是在需要精确知识的医学基准测试中表现出色，同时在数学基准测试中保持竞争力，且可扩展至多种基础模型和参数规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在处理任务时难以平衡组合性（compositionality）与知识性（knowledgeability），即所谓的组合-知识二元对立（Composition-Knowledge Dichotomy）问题，现有方法无法同时兼顾逻辑组织与事实基础。

Method: 提出具体化命题提示（Concretized Proposition Prompting, CPP）框架，该框架显式地将与问题相关的命题进行具体化处理，从而同时增强推理的逻辑性和知识的事实性。

Result: CPP显著提升了推理性能，尤其在需要精确知识的医学基准测试中表现优异，在注重演绎推理的数学基准测试中同样具有竞争力。此外，CPP可扩展至多种基础模型和参数规模。

Conclusion: CPP有效解决了组合-知识二元对立问题，为逻辑组织与事实基础的推理提供了基础性范式，弥合了基于组合与基于知识的方法之间的差距。

Abstract: LLMs often struggle to balance compositionality with knowledgeability, a challenge we define as Composition-Knowledge Dichotomy. To address this, we propose Concretized Proposition Prompting (CPP), a framework that explicitly concretizes propositions relevant to questions. The results demonstrate that CPP significantly enhances reasoning performance, particularly in medical benchmarks where precise knowledge is paramount, while being competitive on math benchmarks where deductive reasoning is prioritized. Additional experiments reveal that CPP is scalable to various foundation models and parameter sizes, being a fundamental paradigm that bridges the gap between composition- and knowledge-based approaches. Consequently, CPP resolves the composition-knowledge dichotomy by providing a solid foundation for logically organized and factually grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [86] [From Prompts to Contracts: Harness Engineering for Auditable Enterprise LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08028)
*Joongho Ahn,Moonsoo Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"框架工程"方法，将企业LLM应用从基于提示的原型转化为可追溯、可审计的架构：确定性行为迁移到代码、清单、模式和验证工件中，围绕可替换的组合边界组织，而基于来源的声明仍作为运行时答案的权威。在韩国5个企业集团（25家上市公司）的数据上验证了该方法在保持来源基础、实体路由、追踪、输出卫生和推荐语言合同方面的有效性，并通过模型替换和消融实验证明代码拥有的保证是关键且不可仅通过提示复现的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业LLM应用通常始于原型，其行为由提示和检索上下文驱动。产品化需要添加源边界、实体路由、答案合同和可重复追踪等要求，但现有原型方法无法满足这些生产级需求。

Method: 提出框架工程方法，将确定性行为从提示中剥离，迁移到代码、清单、模式和验证工件中，围绕可替换的组合边界构建可追溯、可审计的LLM智能体架构。基于来源的声明保留为运行时答案的权威来源。

Result: (1) 框架在固定验证场景中保持了所有合同；故障注入控制确认验证器能标记故意破坏的合同。(2) 框架强制执行的检查在模型替换下保持稳定：3个托管模型共270次组合边界运行全部通过，失败仅限于模型组合侧并被捕获记录。(3) 代码拥有的保证是关键的：仅凭提示指令无法阻止推荐语言和内部追踪泄露违规，框架完全阻止；外部护栏虽可阻止但过度拒绝（88/120 vs 框架120/120）。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可复用的工程模式，将探索性原型转化为可审计的应用，具有版本化的来源、控制和验证工件，在保持安全性的同时不牺牲效用。

Abstract: Enterprise large language model (LLM) applications often begin as prototypes whose behavior is carried by prompts and retrieval context. Productization adds requirements for source boundaries, entity routing, answer contracts, and reproducible traces. We present a harness-engineering approach that reconstructs this pattern into a traceable, auditable LLM-agent architecture: deterministic behavior moves into code, manifests, schemas, and validation artifacts around a replaceable composition boundary, while source-backed claims remain the authority for runtime answers. We instantiate it on a public-data slice of five Korean corporate groups (25 listed companies) and evaluate three research questions. (1) The harness preserves its source-grounding, entity-routing, trace, output-hygiene, and recommendation-language contracts across the fixed validation scenarios; a fault-injection control confirms the validators flag deliberately broken contracts. (2) The checks the harness enforces held under model substitution: across three hosted models, they passed on all 270 composition-boundary runs; failures were confined to the model-composed side and were caught and recorded. (3) The code-owned guarantees are load-bearing, not reproducible by prompting alone: holding the model fixed and varying only the enforcement layer, prompt instructions alone let recommendation-language and internal-trace-leakage violations reach the reader, which the harness blocks entirely. A bolt-on external guardrail prevents such violations too but over-refuses, dropping utility to 88/120 where the harness preserves full utility (120/120); in this ablation, only code-owned enforcement preserves both safety and utility. The result is a reusable engineering pattern for turning exploratory prototypes into auditable applications with versioned source, control, and validation artifacts.

</details>


### [87] [A safety-oriented hypothetico-deductive framework for AI-assisted differential diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08038)
*Fan Ma,Mauro Giuffrè,Donald Wright,Kent McCann,Mark Iscoe,Lingfei Qian,Mingyang Jiang,Chi Wing Ng,Na Hong,Huan He,Cathy Shyr,Qingyu Chen,Lee Schwamm,Lucila Ohno-Machado,Hua Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AegisDx是一个面向安全的假设-演绎临床推理框架，通过角色分工、结构化中间输出、证据检索接口和验证门控机制，在多个医学病例数据集上显著提升了诊断准确性（Top-3准确率提高7-17个百分点）和"不可遗漏"高风险疾病的识别率，并在耶鲁纽黑文医疗系统的急诊病历盲评中获得了临床医师更高的安全评分（4.55 vs 4.31，p=2.1×10⁻⁴）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诊断错误是患者安全的重大威胁，而现有LLM系统通常将诊断视为一次性预测任务，缺乏对高风险遗漏病例的防范机制和推理过程的严格验证。

Method: AegisDx构建安全导向的推理框架，通过角色特定合约（role-specific contracts）协调多个LLM组件，引入结构化中间输出、证据检索接口和验证门控（verification gates），实现广泛鉴别诊断生成、"不可遗漏"高风险疾病的显式筛查、基于医学证据的推理验证以及结构化后续步骤规划。

Result: 在NEJM/JAMA病例上Top-3准确率分别提升11.3和7.8个百分点；在Annals of Emergency Medicine病例上提升17.1个百分点（85.7% vs 68.6%）；"不可遗漏"诊断捕获率从52.0%提升至78.0%；在YNHHS急诊病历盲评中，医师评定的综合安全评分从4.31提升至4.55（调整后p=2.1×10⁻⁴）。

Conclusion: 将诊断AI设计为安全导向的推理框架，而非仅优化预测准确性，能够为急性期临床工作流提供更安全、更透明、更具临床意义的床旁决策支持。

Abstract: Diagnostic error is a major threat to patient safety, yet current large language model (LLM) systems often treat diagnosis as a one-shot prediction task, lacking safeguards against missed high-risk alternatives or rigorous verification of their reasoning. Here, we present AegisDx, a safety-oriented framework for hypothetico-deductive clinical reasoning. AegisDx coordinates specialized LLM components through role-specific contracts, structured intermediate outputs, evidence-retrieval interfaces, and verification gates to generate broad differential diagnoses, enforce explicit screening for dangerous "must-not-miss" conditions, verify reasoning against grounded medical evidence, and structure actionable next steps. We evaluated AegisDx across three layers. On literature-derived case reports from NEJM and JAMA, with GPT-oss-120B as the shared backbone, Top-3 diagnostic accuracy was 59.9% versus 52.1% for the standalone LLM on JAMA cases and 62.7% versus 51.4% on NEJM cases. On cases from Annals of Emergency Medicine, Top-3 accuracy was 85.7% versus 68.6%; against physician-consensus must-not-miss diagnosis sets, AegisDx captured at least one such condition among its top three diagnoses in 78.0% of cases versus 52.0%. In a blinded physician evaluation of 43 real-world emergency department notes from the Yale New Haven Health System compared against GPT-5, AegisDx improved the physician-rated composite safety score from 4.31 to 4.55 on a 5-point scale (adjusted p = 2.1x10^-4), with qualitative gains in must-not-miss identification and reasoning safety. Our findings suggest that engineering diagnostic AI as a safety-oriented reasoning framework, rather than optimizing raw predictive accuracy alone, can provide a safer, more transparent, and clinically meaningful layer of bedside decision support for acute care workflows.

</details>


### [88] [When LLMs Agree, Are They Right? Auditing Self-Consistency and Cross-Model Agreement as Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08065)
*Kaihua Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模跨测试研究（53个测试者，26.5万样本）证明：LLM裁判系统中的一致性（自一致性或交叉一致性）并非准确性的可靠指标，其预测能力弱（rho 0.20-0.59），且在最强前沿模型上表现最差（一致性≥0.8的案例中48%是错误的）。自一致性只是条件性的正确性代理，不能作为独立的置信度分数使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-judge评估系统（包括集成和混合专家陪审团）普遍假设一致性（即评审者之间或模型自身采样之间的共识）等于正确性。这一假设缺乏严格验证，可能导致企业AI管线中评估结果的系统性偏差和过度自信。

Method: 进行大规模跨测试研究：53个测试者针对GPQA Diamond和AIME数据集，对模型层级、提示方式和规模进行交叉比较，每个案例抽取K=50个样本，共计26.5万样本。以多数正确作为部署标签，采用层次化测试者聚类bootstrap方法进行分析。同时进行跨族检查（三个Claude层级），验证前沿模型的过度自信现象。

Result: 一致性与正确性之间的相关性为正但较弱（rho 0.20-0.59）；其有用性依赖于情境：对未饱和的中层模型最佳，对分配计算资源有用；对最一致的前沿模型表现最差（GPQA上77%的案例结果条目一致性≥0.8，其中48%是错误的）；跨族检查显示相同的过度自信模式，置信错误在各提供商间重复出现。

Conclusion: 自一致性只是正确性的条件性代理，不能作为独立的置信度分数使用。研究公开了去标识化的逐测试者行数据和答案分布，为未来评估系统的设计提供了重要警示。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge (Zheng et al., 2023) is increasingly the default for evaluating AI systems in enterprise pipelines, often scaled to ensembles (Verga et al., 2024) or "mixture-of-experts" (Shazeer et al., 2017) panels of judges. These systems share a key assumption: that consistency -- agreement among judges, or among a model's own samples -- indicates correctness. We show this assumption is unreliable. Agreement is not accuracy: a model can agree with itself, and different models can agree with each other, out of shared bias, a memorized heuristic, or an option-position prior rather than truth. We ask when agreement is nonetheless a usable proxy, in a large-scale cross-runner study: 53 runners drew K=50 samples for assigned overlapping cases across comparisons of model tier, prompting, and scale on GPQA Diamond and AIME -- 265,000 samples. Using majority-correctness as the deployment label and a hierarchical runner-clustered bootstrap, agreement is a positive but weak predictor (rho 0.20-0.59, all positive under item-clustered resampling) whose usefulness is regime-dependent: best for unsaturated mid-tier models and for allocating compute, and worst -- over-confident yet no more accurate -- for the most consistent frontier model (agreement >=0.8 on 77% of GPQA case-result entries, 48% of those wrong). An exploratory cross-family check on three Claude tiers shows the same frontier over-confidence, with confident errors recurring across providers above a marginal-preserving null. Self-consistency is thus a conditional proxy for correctness, not a standalone confidence score. We publicly release the de-identified per-run rows and answer distributions.

</details>


### [89] [Persuasion Attacks Can Decrease Effectiveness of CoT Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08066)
*Jennifer Za,Julija Bainiaksina,Nikita Ostrovsky,Tanush Chopra,Victoria Krakovna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoT监控在面对对抗性说服时反而使有害行为批准率增加9.5%；引入跨模型家族的事实检查框架可将违规批准率降低45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT监控在标准场景下有效，但LLM易受基于说服的越狱攻击。作者想检验这种脆弱性是否延伸到监控LLM本身——即对抗性代理能否说服CoT监控器批准违反策略的行为。

Method: 设计了包含40个任务的评估框架，分析了数千次代理-监控器交互。代理被指示为违反策略的提议进行辩论。提出了一种事实检查监控框架，探索同模型与跨模型家族（如Claude 3.7 Sonnet监控器+GPT-4.1事实检查器）的配对效果。

Result: 对抗性设置下，访问CoT推理反而使有害行为批准率平均增加9.5%。跨模型家族的事实检查配对将违规批准率降低45%（同模型配对仅降低6%）。

Conclusion: CoT监控单独不足以防御对抗性说服，模型多样化的事实检查是一种鲁棒的缓解方案。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a promising safety mechanism for AI agents, based on the premise that visible reasoning traces can surface misaligned or deceptive behavior. While effective in standard scenarios, recent work highlights that LLMs remain vulnerable to persuasion-based jailbreaks, where natural-language arguments override model constraints. We stress-test whether this vulnerability extends to monitoring LLMs: can an adversarial agent persuade its CoT monitor to approve proposed actions that violate the monitor's policy? We design an evaluation framework with 40 tasks and analyze thousands of agent-monitor interactions, where agents are instructed to argue for policy-violating proposals. We find that in such adversarial settings, monitor access to the agent's CoT reasoning increases rather than decreases approval of harmful actions on average by 9.5%, as the scratchpad provides an additional persuasion channel. To address this, we introduce a fact-checking monitoring framework. We find that a fact-checker and monitor pairing from different model families, for example a Claude 3.7 Sonnet monitor paired with a GPT-4.1 fact-checker, reduces approval of policy-violating actions by up to 45%, compared to only 6%, when using the same model for both fact-checking and monitoring roles. Our results demonstrate that CoT monitoring alone may be insufficient against adversarial persuasion, and that model-diverse fact-checking provides a robust mitigation.

</details>


### [90] [PARA-PV: Physics-Aware Retrieval-Augmented PV Prediction Based on Frozen Foundation Model and Distribution Shift Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08079)
*Hang Fan,Weican Liu,Ying Lu,Dunnan Liu,Long Cheng,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PARA-PV框架，通过物理感知检索增强、时间序列基础模型校准、分布偏移校正和物理约束损失函数四阶段，实现高精度光伏功率预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏发电受天气变化、昼夜交替、工况依赖动态和严格物理约束共同影响，现有预测方法难以充分融合物理知识，导致预测精度不足。

Method: 1) 物理感知检索增强学习器：检索与当前窗口在时间形状、功率水平、运行状态和日内时段一致的历史片段和类比轨迹；2) 残差适配器：用冻结的Chronos时间序列基础模型校准基础预测；3) 物理感知分布偏移校正模块：利用功率、天气、时间戳和昼夜条件，通过门控均值偏移和尺度校正调整预测；4) 物理约束损失函数：分峰、爬坡、夜间和常规四个区域自适应重加权误差。

Result: 代码已开源，但摘要未提供具体数值结果。

Conclusion: 通过在整个预测流程中嵌入物理知识（检索增强、基础模型校准、分布校正、损失函数约束），可有效提升光伏功率预测的准确性和对关键运行状态的捕获能力。

Abstract: Accurate photovoltaic (PV) power forecasting is essential for reliable grid dispatch and renewable energy integration, yet it remains challenging because PV generation is jointly shaped by weather variability, day-night transitions, regime-dependent dynamics, and strict physical constraints. We propose PARA-PV, a Physics-Aware Retrieval-Augmented framework that embeds physical knowledge throughout the forecasting process. The framework first encodes multivariate PV observations into patch-level representations and, through a physics-aware retrieval-augmented learner, retrieves historical patches and analog trajectories that are consistent with the current window in temporal shape, power level, PV operating state, and intra-day period; this yields a physically grounded base forecast. To supplement local memory with broader temporal knowledge, the base forecast is then calibrated against a frozen Chronos time-series foundation-model prior through a lightweight residual adapter, so that general temporal regularities are adapted to PV-specific dynamics without overriding the physically grounded prediction. Because residual conditional distribution shifts persist when weather and diurnal regimes change, a physics-aware distribution shift correction module subsequently adjusts the preliminary forecast using power, weather, timestamp, and day/night conditions, applying gated mean-shift and scale corrections selectively. Finally, a physics-constrained loss function partitions the samples into peak, ramping, night-time, and regular regimes and adaptively reweights their error contributions, preventing the dominant regular regime from suppressing learning of operationally critical states. Our code is available at https://github.com/weican1103/PARA-PV.

</details>


### [91] [CausalDS: Benchmarking Causal Reasoning in Data-Science Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08093)
*Andrej Leban,Yuekai Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CausalDS是一个评估大语言模型在数据科学工作流中进行因果推理的基准，它通过合成结构因果模型生成带观测数据的场景和自然语言故事，覆盖Pearl三阶梯任务，要求模型具备编码、工具使用、不确定性量化和弃权能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么侧重符号因果推理但缺乏真实数据分析场景，要么侧重数据分析但缺少原则性的因果数据生成结构；同时现有因果评估数据集多来自有限模板化变体的策展示例，缺乏系统性生成的新型合成因果结构。

Method: 每个基准实例通过采样结构因果模型(SCM)生成观测数据，并配以合成自然语言故事；可选地基于真实数据集的经验分布来接地组件；从每个场景派生出覆盖Pearl三阶梯的任务，多数任务包含数据科学编码组件，且存在观测模型生成的不完美观测；将"无法回答时弃权"作为一等评分结果。

Result: CausalDS联合评估了符号因果推理、数据科学、不确定性量化、弃权及工具使用/编码等多方面能力。

Conclusion: CausalDS为评估大语言模型在数据科学智能体工作流中的因果推理提供了一个系统化、可扩展且减少"因果鹦鹉"风险的基准框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as integrated data-science agents, combining abstract reasoning with advanced tool use. Yet the relevant benchmark landscape largely divides into symbolic causal reasoning benchmarks without realistic data analysis or data analysis benchmarks without a principled causal data-generating structure. Furthermore, existing causal evaluation datasets are often restricted to curated examples from existing sources, with diversity coming from limited templatized variations rather than from systematic generation of novel synthetic causal structures. We introduce CausalDS, a benchmark for evaluating causal reasoning in agentic data-science workflows. Each benchmark instance is a scene consisting of a sampled structural causal model (SCM) with generated observational data and an accompanying synthetic natural-language story grounded in a realistic domain. We optionally ground the composition of the benchmark components in empirical distributions obtained from real-world datasets, thus retaining empirical structure while reducing the "causal parrot" risk through completely synthetic generation. From each scene, we then derive tasks spanning all three of Pearl's rungs, with typical data-science prediction tasks appearing as Rung 1. Most tasks include a data science coding component, where the model typically needs to use several tools to arrive at the final answer due to the frequent presence of imperfect observations, which are generated by an observation model. Additionally, recognizing when a question admits no warranted answer and abstaining is treated as a first-class scored outcome. The benchmark thus jointly evaluates symbolic causal reasoning, data science, uncertainty quantification, abstention, and tool use/coding.

</details>


### [92] [Answer Set Programming Energised! End-to-End Neurosymbolic Reasoning and Learning with ASP and Energy Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08136)
*Jakob Suchan,Julius Monsen,Salim Baloch,Mehul Bhatt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于回答集编程（ASP）与基于能量的模型（EBM）模块化集成的神经符号推理与学习方法，支持连续潜空间中的联合优化，并在视觉问答和多目标追踪任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号方法在融合背景知识、约束和非单调推理方面存在不足，且缺乏支持端到端训练的鲁棒平台，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将回答集编程（ASP）与基于能量的模型（EBM）模块化集成，通过显式的ASP声明语义在连续潜空间中进行联合优化，支持背景知识、约束和非单调推理；并推广了回答集模理论，提供ASP为中心、鲁棒的端到端训练框架。

Result: 在MNIST上演示了基本应用，在Clevr视觉问答基准和MOT多目标追踪基准上进行了评估。

Conclusion: 所提方法提供了通用的神经符号推理与学习框架，在动态领域（如感知与交互）中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: We present a general neurosymbolic reasoning and learning methodology based on a modular integration of answer set programming with an energy based model substrate. Key contributions are: (1) supporting joint optimisation in the continuous latent space through explicit ASP-based declarative semantics fully incorporating background knowledge, constraints, non-monotonic inference; and (2) advancing recent works at the interface of answer sets, probabilistic logic, and answer set modulo theories by providing a generalised model and practical platform for ASP-centric robust, end-to-end training for applications in dynamic domains (e.g., involving perception and interaction). We provide a practical implementation, and demonstrate basic use and application (with MNIST), and evaluate with the visual question-answering benchmark Clevr and the multi-object tracking benchmark MOT.

</details>


### [93] [Overthinking: Amplifying Reasoning Weights to Extract Learned Secrets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08173)
*Jack Hopkins,Dipika Khullar,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"过度思考"（overthinking）方法，通过推理任务向量放大推理模型的思考倾向，使非推理基础模型在审计过程中更易暴露隐藏信息。实验表明该方法相比原始推理模型，能使秘密或非预期行为暴露频率提升高达10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的黑盒审计可能遗漏微妙的失调和隐藏信息，现有方法难以有效挖掘这些隐匿内容。

Method: 定义过度思考模型 θ_O_α = θ_M + α(θ_R - θ_M)，其中α>1放大推理；并提出逐层衰减策略，选择性增强推理能力同时保持输出质量与连贯性。

Result: 在四种实验设置、2B-32B参数规模的模型上，过度思考模型比原始推理模型更可能揭示隐藏信息，频率提升高达10倍。

Conclusion: 推理放大可以有效促使模型表面训练中获得的秘密或非预期行为，但秘密暴露方式因类型而异：部分需要沿推理方向扰动，另一些则对任意足够大的权重扰动均敏感。

Abstract: Black box auditing of language models is an essential pre-deployment tool, but it may miss subtle forms of misalignment and hidden information. To better elicit hidden information during an auditing process, we introduce \emph{overthinking}: the process of using reasoning task vectors to amplify the propensity to think out loud of reasoning models. Given the parameters of a non-reasoning instruct model $M$ and reasoning-distilled model $R$, we define the \emph{overthinking model} as $\boldsymbolθ_{\mathcal{O}_α} = \boldsymbolθ_{\mathcal{M}} + α(\boldsymbolθ_{\mathcal{R}} - \boldsymbolθ_{\mathcal{M}})$, where $α> 1$ amplifies reasoning beyond the pure reasoning model $R$. Additionally, we introduce new layer-wise attenuation strategies that selectively amplify reasoning without losing quality and coherence of model outputs. We demonstrate that overthinking models are more likely to reveal hidden information across four experimental settings, across 2B-32B models. Our findings suggest that reasoning amplification may surface secrets or unintended behaviors acquired during training up to $10\times$ more frequently than the original reasoning model. How secrets surface depends on the secret type: some require perturbation along the reasoning direction, while others yield to any sufficiently large weight perturbation.

</details>


### [94] [ASMR: Agentic Schema Generation for Ship Maintenance Report Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08177)
*Sohrab Namazi Nia,Amogh Dalal,Ning Sa,Peter Ly,Marti Zentmaier,Tomek Strzalkowski,Jay Miller,Rishi Singh,Senjuti Basu Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ASMR框架，通过两个智能代理（字段生成代理和结构优化代理）自动从历史船舶维护和运营报告中生成紧凑且信息丰富的模式，以指导报告编写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给定多个类别的历史船舶维护和运营报告集合，需要自动发现能够捕获每类报告核心信息需求的紧凑且信息丰富的模式，以提升报告质量。

Method: ASMR模块化代理框架包含两个专用代理：字段生成代理通过自适应多粒度聚类从历史叙述中提取语义概念并生成候选模式字段；结构优化代理使用强化学习识别紧凑、信息丰富且无冗余的模式表示。

Result: 初步结果展示了所提方法的前景，并指出了数据管理、代理AI和人机交互AI交叉领域的若干开放研究挑战。

Conclusion: ASMR框架能够自动生成模式以指导报告作者产出更完整、一致且可操作的报告，但该领域仍存在多个开放研究问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the automatic schema generation problem: given a collection of historical ship maintenance and operational reports across multiple form categories, automatically discover compact and informative schemas that capture the essential information requirements of each report type. To address this challenge, we propose ASMR, a modular agentic framework consisting of two specialized agents. A Field Generation Agent extracts semantic concepts from historical narratives and generates candidate schema fields through adaptive multi-granularity clustering, while a Structural Optimizer Agent employs reinforcement learning to identify compact, informative, and non-redundant schema representations. The resulting schemas can guide report authors toward producing more complete, consistent, and actionable reports. Preliminary results demonstrate the promise of the proposed approach and highlight several open research challenges at the intersection of data management, agentic AI, and human-centered AI.

</details>


### [95] [A First-Principles Theory of Slow Thinking and Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08196)
*Hongkang Yang,Zhi-Qin John Xu,Feiyu Xiong,Weinan E*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"主动提升"理论，通过概率分布的提升与投影以及不确定性最小化驱动，为慢思考（主动感知）提供数学基础，推导出慢思考大语言模型的设计、训练与推理框架，并建立了表征层级与采样层级体系，可用于升级现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为认知功能提供第一性原理建模，特别是为"慢思考"（主动感知）提供严格的数学形式化框架，并将其应用于大语言模型的设计、训练和推理中。

Method: 通过概率分布在可观测量空间与潜空间上的提升与投影，以简单函数族（如神经网络）表示复杂数据分布；提出"主动提升"理论，基于潜序列采样和以最大速率降低不确定性的内在驱动力，推导出设计空间、静态理论、推理过程（含内时间轴）及类最小编码长度的训练目标。

Result: 推导出包含慢思考模型在内的庞大设计空间（静态理论子空间）；建立了表征层级与采样层级体系；得到具有内时间轴的推理过程和类似最小长度编码的训练目标；产生若干技术副产品：改进慢思考模型的三阶段路径、统一的多模态编码器与生成模型构建方法、人眼视觉表征的先验形成、策略坍塌的可能解决方案。

Conclusion: 主动提升理论从第一性原理刻画了感知的能动性（包括慢思考格式的产生），为认知功能的数学建模提供了统一框架，并衍生出多种可落地的技术成果。

Abstract: As part of a series on first-principles modeling of cognitive functions, this paper attempts to provide a mathematical formulation of thinking and perception. It formally derives slow thinking or more generally, active perception, and encompasses the design, training and inference of slow thinking large language models. Our starting point is the lifting and projection of probability distributions on the observable and latent spaces, with the objective of representing complex data distributions by simple function families such as neural networks. A theory called "active lifting" is proposed, based on the sampling of latent sequences and an intrinsic drive to reduce uncertainty with maximum rate. It derives a large design space, containing the slow thinking models in a subspace that we call the static theory. These models are positioned on the representation hierarchy and sampler hierarchy induced by the static theory, and can be upgraded by climbing the two hierarchies. Active lifting further derives an inference process with an internal time axis, and a training objective that resembles minimum-length coding as well as the invention of languages. Thus, it characterizes the agency of perception, including the emergence of the slow thinking formats. Technical by-products of this theory include a three-stage pathway for improving slow thinking models, a unified approach to constructing encoders and generative models for all data modalities, a priori formation of human-like visual representations, and a possible solution to policy collapse.

</details>


### [96] [Playing ZendoWorld: Challenging AI Agents on Active Visual Concept Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08233)
*Sophia Koehler,Antonia Wüst,Inga Ibs,Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Wolfgang Stammer,Constantin Rothkopf,Kevin Ellis,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZendoWorld是一个受控交互环境，用于评估智能体在视觉游戏中推断逻辑规则、主动设计实验和更新假设的能力。研究发现：(1)高标签预测准确率不等同于规则恢复；(2)感知和归纳推理对不同智能体是独立瓶颈；(3)VLM智能体无法提出信息性实验。人类数据表明在复杂规则上存在归纳推理差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能够联合感知复杂输入、形成关于隐藏模式的假设并设计信息性实验来检验假设的智能系统是人工智能的核心挑战，目前缺乏标准化的评估环境来衡量这些能力。

Method: 提出ZendoWorld交互环境，要求智能体从视觉观测中推断逻辑规则，通过提出新场景获取信息，并根据环境反馈更新假设。评估了纯VLM推理、贝叶斯粒子滤波、动态概念发现和神经符号方法等多种智能体，并收集了人类数据作为对比。

Result: (1)高准确率预测标签不等于恢复了底层规则；(2)感知和归纳推理是不同智能体类别的独立瓶颈；(3)VLM智能体提出的实验近乎无信息量，无法主动减少假设不确定性。人类在复杂规则上同样存在归纳推理差距。

Conclusion: ZendoWorld为评估智能体在科学发现等领域的主动推理和实验设计能力提供了重要基准，并指明了具体的改进方向。

Abstract: A central challenge in building intelligent systems is enabling agents to jointly perceive complex inputs, form hypotheses about hidden patterns, and design informative experiments to test them. To study this problem, we propose ZendoWorld, a controlled interactive environment in which agents must infer a logical rule about visual game observations, acquire information by proposing new scenes, and refine their hypotheses based on feedback from the game environment. We evaluate several agents spanning pure VLM reasoning, Bayesian particle filtering, dynamic concept discovery, and neuro-symbolic methods. Our main findings are: (1) high accuracy in predicting labels for observed examples does not imply recovery of the underlying rule; (2) perception and induction are distinct bottlenecks for different agent classes; and (3) VLM-based agents propose near-uninformative experiments, failing to actively reduce hypothesis uncertainty. To compare these results, we collect human data on the task, which reveals a gap in inductive reasoning, particularly for more complex rules. Overall, ZENDOWORLD takes an important step toward evaluating intelligent agents and identifies concrete avenues for improvement, particularly in domains like scientific discovery.

</details>
